"""Display, probes and saved artifacts use the geometry the solve ran on.

A run-time (or capacity-workflow) imperfection moves the reference coordinates
of a copy of the model; the displacements are measured from that copy.  The
deformed shape must therefore be "imperfect + displacement", and a saved
artifact must carry that reference so reopening it draws the same shape.
Runs without an imperfection, and project imperfections applied at build time,
keep drawing on the mesh coordinates bit for bit.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from anyfem import eigenmode_imperfection, solve_capacity, solve_nonlinear_static
from anyfem.io.artifacts import ArtifactStore
from anyfem.io.result_artifact import write_solution_artifact
from anyfem.ui.scene import build_persisted_result_scene, build_result_scene

from test_imperfect_solved_geometry import (  # noqa: F401
    AMPLITUDE,
    compressed_plate,
    plate_and_mode,
)


def _fe_coordinates(model, node_ids):
    return np.array([[n.x, n.y, n.z] for n in (model.mesh.get_node(i) for i in node_ids)])


def _scene_positions(scene):
    return np.concatenate(
        [np.asarray(p, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 3) for patch in scene.faces for p in patch.polygons]
    )


def _runtime(plate_and_mode):
    project, mesh, buckling = plate_and_mode
    imperfection = eigenmode_imperfection(buckling, 1, amplitude=AMPLITUDE)
    return solve_nonlinear_static(
        project, mesh=mesh, imperfection=imperfection, max_load_factor=1.0, num_steps=1,
    )


def _capacity(plate_and_mode):
    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    return solve_capacity(
        project, mesh=mesh, num_buckling_modes=1, imperfection_amplitude=AMPLITUDE,
        num_steps=1, max_load_factor=1.0,
    )


def _store(tmp_path, solution):
    project = solution.built.project
    store = ArtifactStore(tmp_path / "plate.anyfem")
    identity = dict(mesh_id="mesh", document_id=project.document_id,
                    model_hash="model", mesh_hash="hash")
    store.write_mesh(solution.built.mesh, **identity)
    artifact = write_solution_artifact(store, solution, job_id="job", analysis_hash="a", **identity)
    return store, artifact


@pytest.mark.parametrize("route", ["nonlinear", "capacity"])
def test_deformed_positions_are_imperfect_reference_plus_displacement(plate_and_mode, route):
    solution = (_runtime if route == "nonlinear" else _capacity)(plate_and_mode)
    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
    reference = _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)
    assert np.max(np.abs(reference - solution.built.mesh.node_positions())) > 0.5 * AMPLITUDE

    np.testing.assert_array_equal(solution.reference_positions(), reference)
    expected = reference + 3.0 * solution.translations()
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(solution.deformed_positions(3.0), expected)

    scene = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
    by_node = dict(zip(ids, expected))
    first = next(iter(solution.built.mesh.shells))
    corners = np.array([by_node[n] for n in solution.built.mesh.corners_of(first)])
    polygons = [np.asarray(poly) for patch in scene.faces for poly in patch.polygons]
    assert any(poly.shape == corners.shape and np.array_equal(poly, corners) for poly in polygons)


def test_csv_positions_use_the_reference_geometry(plate_and_mode):
    from anyfem.post.report import field_to_csv

    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
    reference = dict(zip(ids, _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)))
    rows = [line.split(",") for line in field_to_csv(solution, "magnitude").splitlines()[1:]]
    assert rows
    for row in rows:
        np.testing.assert_allclose([float(v) for v in row[1:4]], reference[int(row[0])], atol=1e-8)


def test_artifact_records_the_reference_geometry_and_reopens_on_it(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
    reference = _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)

    assert dataset.metadata("provenance")["reference_geometry"]["kind"] == "solved_imperfect"
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.asarray(dataset.table("reference_node_ids")), ids)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.asarray(dataset.table("reference_node_positions")), reference)

    scene = build_persisted_result_scene(
        solution.built.project, solution.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=3.0
    )
    live = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(_scene_positions(scene), _scene_positions(live))


def test_ordinary_artifact_names_the_mesh_and_gains_no_table(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    solution = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
    assert dataset.metadata("provenance")["reference_geometry"]["kind"] == "mesh_record"
    assert "reference_node_positions" not in dataset.table_keys
    scene = build_persisted_result_scene(
        project, solution.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=3.0
    )
    live = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(_scene_positions(scene), _scene_positions(live))


def test_artifact_without_a_reference_record_reads_as_the_mesh(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    from anyfem.ui.scene import persisted_reference_positions

    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    solution = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)

    class Old:  # an artifact written before the reference record existed
        table_keys = tuple(k for k in dataset.table_keys if not k.startswith("reference_"))
        table = staticmethod(dataset.table)

        @staticmethod
        def metadata(name):
            old = dict(dataset.metadata(name))
            old.pop("reference_geometry", None)
            return old

    ordered = sorted(mesh.nodes)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
        persisted_reference_positions(Old, mesh, ordered), mesh.node_positions()
    )


def test_unbound_runtime_imperfection_refuses_the_display(plate_and_mode):
    from anyfem.post.results import NonlinearSolution, UnboundSolvedGeometryError

    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    unbound = NonlinearSolution(
        displacements=solution.displacements, built=solution.built,
        raw_result=solution.raw_result, info=dict(solution.info),
    )
    with pytest.raises(UnboundSolvedGeometryError):
        unbound.deformed_positions(1.0)


def test_ordinary_and_build_time_displays_are_unchanged(plate_and_mode):
    from anyfem.model.imperfections import Imperfection

    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    plain = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
        plain.deformed_positions(2.0), mesh.node_positions() + 2.0 * plain.translations()
    )
    p2, face = compressed_plate()
    p2.imperfections.append(Imperfection(name="dent", ref=p2.face(face), amplitude=AMPLITUDE,
                                         kind="plate_mode", direction=(0.0, 0.0, 1.0)))
    m2 = p2.generate_mesh(0.25)
    built_time = solve_nonlinear_static(p2, mesh=m2, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    # Pinned scope: build-time imperfections keep drawing on the mesh record.
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
        built_time.deformed_positions(2.0), m2.node_positions() + 2.0 * built_time.translations()
    )


def test_artifact_of_an_unbound_imperfect_result_refuses_its_reference(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    from anyfem.post.results import NonlinearSolution

    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    unbound = NonlinearSolution(

        )

class _Patched:
    """A real dataset with edited provenance or tables."""

    def __init__(self, dataset, reference=None, tables=None):
        self._d, self._reference, self._tables = dataset, reference, tables or {}

    def __getattr__(self, name):
        return getattr(self._d, name)

    def metadata(self, name):
        data = dict(self._d.metadata(name))
        if name == "provenance" and self._reference is not None:
            data["reference_geometry"] = self._reference
        return data

    def table(self, key, *args):
        return self._tables[key] if key in self._tables else self._d.table(key, *args)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("reference", [{"kind": "future"}, {}, "text"])
def test_unrecognised_reference_record_is_refused(plate_and_mode, tmp_path, reference):
    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = _Patched(store.open_result(artifact), reference=reference)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unrecognised"):
        build_persisted_result_scene(
            solution.built.project, solution.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=1.0
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("damage", ["duplicate", "shape", "fractional"])
def test_malformed_stored_reference_is_refused(plate_and_mode, tmp_path, damage):
    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    real = store.open_result(artifact)
    ids = np.asarray(real.table("reference_node_ids"))
    xyz = np.asarray(real.table("reference_node_positions"))
    tables = {
        "duplicate": {"reference_node_ids": np.concatenate([ids[:-1], ids[-2:-1]])},
        "shape": {"reference_node_positions": xyz[:-1]},
        "fractional": {"reference_node_ids": ids + 0.5},
    }[damage]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="malformed"):
        build_persisted_result_scene(
            solution.built.project, solution.built.mesh, _Patched(real, tables=tables),
            "displacement", scale=1.0,
        )


def test_capacity_stage_fields_stay_on_the_perfect_geometry(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    from anyfem.ui.scene import persisted_reference_positions

    solution = _capacity(plate_and_mode)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
    assert "static_displacement" in dataset.field_keys
    mesh = solution.built.mesh
    ordered = sorted(mesh.nodes)
    perfect = np.asarray([mesh.nodes[n] for n in ordered], dtype=float)
    # Reference static and elastic buckling ran on the unmoved model.
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
        persisted_reference_positions(dataset, mesh, ordered, perfect_stage=True), perfect
    )
    assert not np.array_equal(persisted_reference_positions(dataset, mesh, ordered), perfect)

     if refusal is not None:
         raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(refusal)

+
+
+def solved_reference_positions(view: Any) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
+    """Reference coordinates of a result solved on moved geometry, else ``None``.
+
+    ``None`` means the ordinary route: the mesh coordinates are the reference,
+    untouched.  Otherwise the rows follow ``sorted(built.mesh.nodes)`` and come
+    from the bound solved model; a refused or missing binding raises.
+    """
+
+    geometry = getattr(view, "solved_geometry", None)
+    if geometry is None:
+        refusal = unbound_refusal(view)
+        if refusal is not None:
+            raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(refusal)
+        return None
+    model = geometry.model()
+    get_node = model.mesh.get_node
+    rows = []
+    for node_id in sorted(view.built.mesh.nodes):
+        node = get_node(node_id)
+        if node is None:
+            raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(
+                f"the solved model has no node {node_id}; its geometry does not match the mesh"
+            )
+        rows.append((node.x, node.y, node.z))
+    return np.asarray(rows, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 3)
+
+
+def reference_position_map(view: Any, mesh: Any) -> Any:
+    """``node id -> xyz`` of the reference geometry: ``mesh.nodes`` unless moved."""
+
+    positions = solved_reference_positions(view)
+    if positions is None:
+        return mesh.nodes
+    return {node_id: positions[row] for row, node_id in enumerate(sorted(mesh.nodes))}

+_PERFECT_STAGES = frozenset({"reference_static", "elastic_buckling"})
+
+
+def _reference_record(dataset):
+    """``(kind, record)`` of the saved reference geometry; absent means the mesh."""
+
+    try:
+        provenance = dataset.metadata("provenance")
+    except (KeyError, AttributeError):
+        provenance = None
+    if not isinstance(provenance, Mapping) or "reference_geometry" not in provenance:
+        return "mesh_record", {}
+    record = provenance["reference_geometry"]
+    kind = record.get("kind") if isinstance(record, Mapping) else None
+    if kind not in ("mesh_record", "solved_imperfect", "unbound_imperfect"):
+        raise ValueError(f"unrecognised saved reference geometry {record!r}")
+    return kind, record
+
+
+def persisted_reference_positions(dataset, mesh, ordered_nodes, *, perfect_stage=False) -> np.ndarray:
+    """Coordinates a saved result's displacements are measured from.
+
+    The mesh record unless the artifact says it was solved on moved geometry,
+    in which case the stored imperfect coordinates are used.  Artifacts written
+    before the record existed have no key and read as the mesh.  An imperfect
+    result whose geometry was not saved, or a record this reader does not
+    understand, is refused.  ``perfect_stage`` marks a capacity stage that was
+    solved on the unmoved model (reference static, elastic buckling).
+    """
+
+    kind, record = _reference_record(dataset)
+    if kind == "unbound_imperfect":
+        raise ValueError(
+            "this result was solved on imperfect geometry that its artifact does not "
+            "store; the deformed shape is not drawn on the perfect mesh"
+        )
+    if kind == "solved_imperfect" and not perfect_stage:
+        ids = np.asarray(dataset.table(record["ids_table"]))
+        stored = np.asarray(dataset.table(record["positions_table"]), dtype=float)
+        if (
+            ids.ndim != 1 or ids.dtype.kind not in "iu" or stored.shape != (len(ids), 3)
+            or len(set(ids.tolist())) != len(ids) or not np.isfinite(stored).all()
+        ):
+            raise ValueError("the saved reference geometry is malformed; it is not used")
+        row_of = {int(node): row for row, node in enumerate(ids)}
+        try:
+            return stored[[row_of[int(node)] for node in ordered_nodes]]
+        except KeyError as error:
+            raise ValueError(
+                f"the saved reference geometry has no node {error.args[0]}; "
+                "it does not match the result's mesh"
+            ) from None
+    return np.asarray([mesh.nodes[node] for node in ordered_nodes], dtype=float)
+

def read_persisted_display_field(dataset,field_key,*,frame=0,component=None):
    from ..post.fields import Field
    stored = dataset.field(field_key)
    descriptor = stored.descriptor
    local_frame=_persisted_frame(stored,frame)
    if component is not None and component not in descriptor.components:
        raise ValueError(f"{descriptor.label} has no saved component {component!r}")
    if component is not None:
        values = stored.read_component(component,local_frame)
        scalar = _artifact_scalar_rows(values,(),None,descriptor.provenance.get("scalar_sample_axes"))
    else:
        values = stored.read(local_frame)
        scalar = _artifact_scalar_rows(values,descriptor.components,None,descriptor.provenance.get("scalar_sample_axes"))
    location = descriptor.location
    if location not in ("node", "element", "integration_point"):
        raise ValueError(f"{descriptor.label} is not a spatial contour quantity")
    table_kind = "node" if location == "node" else "element"
    table_key = f"{field_key}_{table_kind}_ids"
    if table_key not in dataset.table_keys:
        raise ValueError(f"{descriptor.label} has no persisted {table_kind} association")
    identifiers = np.asarray(dataset.table(table_key), dtype=int).reshape(-1)
    if len(scalar) != len(identifiers):
        raise ValueError(
            f"{descriptor.label} has {len(scalar)} values for {len(identifiers)} IDs"
        )
    mapping = {
        int(identifier): float(value)
        for identifier, value in zip(identifiers, scalar)
        if np.isfinite(value)
    }
    field = Field(
        name=field_key,
        unit=descriptor.unit,
        node_values=mapping if table_kind == "node" else {},
        element_values=mapping if table_kind == "element" else {},
        reduction="absolute single component" if component is None and len(descriptor.components)==1 and values.ndim>1 else descriptor.reduction,
    )

    return field,descriptor


_PERFECT_STAGES = frozenset({"reference_static", "elastic_buckling"})


def _reference_record(dataset):
    """``(kind, record)`` of the saved reference geometry; absent means the mesh."""

    try:
        provenance = dataset.metadata("provenance")
    except (KeyError, AttributeError):
        provenance = None
    if not isinstance(provenance, Mapping) or "reference_geometry" not in provenance:
        return "mesh_record", {}
    record = provenance["reference_geometry"]
    kind = record.get("kind") if isinstance(record, Mapping) else None
    if kind not in ("mesh_record", "solved_imperfect", "unbound_imperfect"):
        raise ValueError(f"unrecognised saved reference geometry {record!r}")
    return kind, record


def persisted_reference_positions(dataset, mesh, ordered_nodes, *, perfect_stage=False) -> np.ndarray:
    """Coordinates a saved result's displacements are measured from.

    The mesh record unless the artifact says it was solved on moved geometry,
    in which case the stored imperfect coordinates are used.  Artifacts written
    before the record existed have no key and read as the mesh.  An imperfect
    result whose geometry was not saved, or a record this reader does not
    understand, is refused.  ``perfect_stage`` marks a capacity stage that was
    solved on the unmoved model (reference static, elastic buckling).
    """

    kind, record = _reference_record(dataset)
    if kind == "unbound_imperfect":
        raise ValueError(
            "this result was solved on imperfect geometry that its artifact does not "
            "store; the deformed shape is not drawn on the perfect mesh"
        )
    if kind == "solved_imperfect" and not perfect_stage:
        ids = np.asarray(dataset.table(record["ids_table"]))
        stored = np.asarray(dataset.table(record["positions_table"]), dtype=float)
        if (
            ids.ndim != 1 or ids.dtype.kind not in "iu" or stored.shape != (len(ids), 3)
            or len(set(ids.tolist())) != len(ids) or not np.isfinite(stored).all()
        ):
            raise ValueError("the saved reference geometry is malformed; it is not used")
        row_of = {int(node): row for row, node in enumerate(ids)}
        try:
            return stored[[row_of[int(node)] for node in ordered_nodes]]
        except KeyError as error:
            raise ValueError(
                f"the saved reference geometry has no node {error.args[0]}; "
                "it does not match the result's mesh"
            ) from None
    return np.asarray([mesh.nodes[node] for node in ordered_nodes], dtype=float)


def build_persisted_result_scene(
    project: Project,
    mesh: Mesh,
    dataset,
    field_key: str,
    *,
    frame: int = 0,
    component: str | None = None,
    scale: float = 1.0,
    limits: Optional[tuple[float, float]] = None,
    colormap: Optional[Sequence[tuple[float, str]]] = None,
    display_units: str = "SI (m / Pa)",
    show_nodes: bool = False,
    show_beam_sections: bool = True,
) -> Scene:
    """Render one lazily-read artifact field without inventing quantities."""

    from ..post.fields import Field

    field,descriptor = read_persisted_display_field(dataset,field_key,frame=frame,component=component)
    ordered_nodes = sorted(mesh.nodes)
    perfect_stage = descriptor.provenance.get("capacity_stage") in _PERFECT_STAGES
    positions = persisted_reference_positions(
        dataset, mesh, ordered_nodes, perfect_stage=perfect_stage
    )
    # A stage solved on the unmoved model is not deformed by the collapse
    # displacement of a result whose later stages ran on moved geometry.
    deform = not (perfect_stage and _reference_record(dataset)[0] == "solved_imperfect")
    if deform and "displacement" in dataset.field_keys and scale != 0.0:
        displacement = dataset.field("displacement")
        displacement_key = "displacement_node_ids"
        if displacement_key in dataset.table_keys:
            displacement_values = np.asarray(
                displacement.read(_persisted_frame(displacement,frame)),
                dtype=float,
            )
            displacement_ids = np.asarray(
                dataset.table(displacement_key), dtype=int
            ).reshape(-1)
            labels=tuple(displacement.descriptor.components)
            if displacement_values.ndim >= 2 and {"ux","uy","uz"}<=set(labels):
                translation_indices=[labels.index(label) for label in ("ux","uy","uz")]
                by_node = {
                    int(node): np.take(row,translation_indices,axis=-1).reshape(-1, 3)[0]
                    for node, row in zip(displacement_ids, displacement_values)
                }
                positions = positions + scale * np.asarray(
                    [by_node.get(int(node), np.zeros(3)) for node in ordered_nodes],
                    dtype=float,
                ).reshape(-1, 3)

    shape = SimpleNamespace(
        built=SimpleNamespace(mesh=mesh, project=project),
        deformed_positions=lambda _scale=1.0: positions,
    )
    scene = build_result_scene(
        shape,
        field=field_key,
        scale=1.0,
        limits=limits,
        values=field,
        colormap=colormap,
        display_units=display_units,
        show_nodes=show_nodes,
        show_beam_sections=show_beam_sections,
    )
    _,_,scene.legend=persisted_display_metadata(field,descriptor,component,limits=limits,display_units=display_units,colormap=colormap)
    return scene


def _artifact_frame(shape: Sequence[int], frame: int) -> int | None:
    if not shape:
        return None
    return min(max(int(frame), 0), int(shape[0]) - 1)

def _persisted_frame(stored,frame):
    resolver=getattr(stored,"root_frame_index",None)
    return resolver(frame) if callable(resolver) else _artifact_frame(stored.shape,frame)


def _artifact_scalar_rows(values, components, component, sample_axes=None):
    from anypost import vector_magnitude, absolute_sample_max
    array = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
    if array.ndim <= 1:
        return array.reshape(-1)
    if component and component in components:
        array = np.take(array, components.index(component), axis=-1)
    elif components and array.shape[-1] == len(components):
        labels=("ux","uy","uz") if {"ux","uy","uz"}<=set(components) else tuple(components)
        indices=[components.index(label) for label in labels]
        array = vector_magnitude(np.take(array,indices,axis=-1), components=labels)
    return absolute_sample_max(array,nan_policy="omit") if array.ndim > 1 else array


def result_display_metadata(field, *, limits=None, display_units="SI (m / Pa)", colormap=None):
    """Legend/range only; no geometry or owner reconstruction."""
"""The reference geometry a nonlinear solve actually ran on.

Project imperfections are applied while the model is built, so they already
live in ``built.fe_model``.  A run-time ``imperfection`` passed to the
nonlinear-static or arc-length solve, and the capacity workflow's eigenmode
imperfection, are applied by the solver to a *copy* of that model.  The
displacements are measured from the copy's coordinates, so every recovery
that combines them with geometry -- element frames, strain-displacement
operators, transverse shear -- has to use the copy as well.  Recovering on the
perfect model mixes two geometries and silently returns wrong stresses.

:class:`SolvedGeometry` binds a result to that solved model.  It is exact or
it refuses:

* the capacity workflow returns the very model it solved, which is kept as is;
* for nonlinear static and arc length the solver keeps its copy to itself.
  ANYfem converts the imperfection once into an owned snapshot, hands that
  snapshot to the solver, checks it against the solver's provenance record
  after the solve, and immediately makes the same copy through the solver's
  own :func:`apply_imperfection` (bit-identical coordinates, and the model
  state as it was when solved).  A disagreement is a typed refusal, not a
  plausible number.

The copy costs one model deep copy after an imperfect solve; runs without an
imperfection pay nothing.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, List, Mapping, Optional

import numpy as np

from ..model.project import ProjectError

__all__ = [
    "SolvedGeometry",
    "UnboundSolvedGeometryError",
    "owned_imperfection_field",
    "reference_position_map",
    "solved_reference_positions",
    "solved_model_of",
    "unbound_refusal",
]


class UnboundSolvedGeometryError(ProjectError):
    """A result's solved geometry is unknown or disagrees with its solver record."""


def _trusted_imperfection(imperfection: Any) -> bool:
    """Plain data or the solver's own pure converters, checked by exact type.

    Only these are converted outside the solver.  A caller-defined converter,
    or a subclass of a mapping or field, may run code, return different
    offsets on another call or mutate the model; it is therefore handed to the
    solver unchanged so the solver's own dispatch, validation and mutation
    guard still apply -- and its geometry is not reproduced for recovery.
    """

    from anyfesolver.imperfections import (
        CompositeImperfection,
        EigenmodeImperfection,
        ImperfectionField,
        StandardImperfection,
    )

    # Trust is decided by exact built-in types all the way down, without
    # iterating anything that is not a built-in container: a custom mapping,
    # sequence or array-like can run code when read, and a one-shot iterator
    # would be consumed by the check itself.
    kind = type(imperfection)
    if kind is dict:
        return _plain_offsets(imperfection)
    if kind is ImperfectionField:
        return _plain_offsets(imperfection.offsets) and type(imperfection.metadata) is dict
    if kind is EigenmodeImperfection:
        return type(imperfection.buckling_result).__module__.startswith("anyfesolver.")
    if kind is StandardImperfection:
        return type(imperfection.node_ids) in (tuple, list) and all(
            type(node) is int for node in imperfection.node_ids
        )
    if kind is CompositeImperfection:
        return type(imperfection.components) in (tuple, list) and all(
            _trusted_imperfection(part) for part in imperfection.components
        )
    return False


_PLAIN_NUMBERS = (int, float, np.float64, np.float32, np.int64, np.int32)


def _plain_offsets(offsets: Any) -> bool:
    if type(offsets) is not dict:
        return False
    for node, offset in offsets.items():
        if type(node) not in (int, np.int64, np.int32):
            return False
        if type(offset) is np.ndarray:
            if offset.dtype.kind not in "fiu":
                return False
        elif type(offset) in (tuple, list):
            if not all(type(value) in _PLAIN_NUMBERS for value in offset):
                return False
        else:
            return False
    return True


def owned_imperfection_field(base_model: Any, imperfection: Any):
    """An immutable snapshot of ``imperfection`` as the solver will see it.

    Returns ``None`` for an input that is not :func:`_trusted_imperfection`;
    the caller must then pass the original object to the solver and treat the
    solved geometry as not reproducible.  Otherwise the conversion follows the
    solver's own owned-input snapshot: a plain mapping is the offsets (name
    ``"imperfection"``, no metadata), a field keeps its name and metadata, and
    a converter is called once against ``base_model`` -- the model state the
    solver converts against.  Offsets become float64 copies keyed by integer
    node id.
    """

    from anyfesolver.imperfections import ImperfectionField

    if not _trusted_imperfection(imperfection):
        return None
    if type(imperfection) is dict:
        offsets, name, metadata = imperfection, "imperfection", {}
    else:
        field = (
            imperfection
            if type(imperfection) is ImperfectionField
            else imperfection.to_field(base_model)
        )
        if type(field) is not ImperfectionField or not isinstance(field.offsets, Mapping) \
                or not isinstance(field.metadata, Mapping):
            return None
        offsets, name, metadata = field.offsets, field.name, field.metadata
    owned = {
        int(node_id): np.asarray(offset, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
        for node_id, offset in offsets.items()
    }
    return ImperfectionField(owned, name=str(name), metadata=dict(metadata))


def _has_offset(imperfection_field: Any) -> bool:
    # ``!= 0`` is also true for NaN, so a non-finite field is never treated as
    # the perfect geometry.
    return any(np.any(offset != 0.0) for offset in imperfection_field.offsets.values())


def _records(entries: Any) -> List[Mapping[str, Any]]:
    if entries is None:
        return []
    return [entry for entry in entries if isinstance(entry, Mapping)]


def _same_record(left: Mapping[str, Any], right: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    return (
        str(left.get("name")) == str(right.get("name"))
        and float(left.get("max_offset", np.nan)) == float(right.get("max_offset", np.nan))
    )


def _solver_imperfection_records(raw_result: Any) -> Optional[List[Mapping[str, Any]]]:
    info = getattr(raw_result, "info", None)
    if not isinstance(info, Mapping) or "imperfection" not in info:
        return None
    return _records(info.get("imperfection"))


class SolvedGeometry:
    """The FE model a result's displacements are measured from, or a refusal."""

    __slots__ = ("_model", "_refusal")

    def __init__(self, *, model: Any = None, refusal: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        if (model is None) == (refusal is None):
            raise ValueError("SolvedGeometry needs exactly one of a model or a refusal")
        self._model = model
        self._refusal = refusal

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @classmethod
    def exact(cls, model: Any) -> "SolvedGeometry":
        """Bind to the model object the solver returned as solved."""

        return cls(model=model)

    @classmethod
    def refused(cls, reason: str) -> "SolvedGeometry":
        """A binding that refuses recovery, e.g. for views of an unbound result."""

        return cls(refusal=str(reason))

    @classmethod
    def rebuild(cls, base_model: Any, imperfection_field: Any, raw_result: Any,
                *, context: str) -> Optional["SolvedGeometry"]:
        """Bind to ``base_model`` plus the run-time imperfection the solver applied.

        Call this straight after the solve, with the owned snapshot from
        :func:`owned_imperfection_field` that was also the object handed to the
        solver -- so solve and recovery consume one conversion.  The copy is
        made here, eagerly, so later edits to the built model (coordinates,
        thickness, materials, connectivity) cannot leak into it: it is the same
        deep copy of the same model state the solver made, moved by the same
        offsets through the same :func:`apply_imperfection`.  Returns ``None``
        when every offset is exactly zero: the solved coordinates are then the
        base coordinates bit for bit, and the ordinary route stays untouched.
        """

        from anyfesolver.imperfections import ImperfectionField

        if not isinstance(imperfection_field, ImperfectionField):
            raise TypeError("rebuild needs the owned ImperfectionField given to the solver")
        records = _solver_imperfection_records(raw_result)
        build_records = _records(getattr(base_model, "imperfection_metadata", None))
        expected = {"name": imperfection_field.name, "max_offset": imperfection_field.max_offset}
        if (
            records is None
            or len(records) != len(build_records) + 1
            or not all(_same_record(a, b) for a, b in zip(records, build_records))
            or not _same_record(records[-1], expected)
        ):
            raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(
                f"{context}: the solver's imperfection record {records!r} does not "
                f"match the build-time imperfections {build_records!r} plus the "
                f"run-time imperfection {expected!r}; the solved geometry cannot be "
                "reproduced, so no stresses are recovered on it"
            )
        if not _has_offset(imperfection_field):
            return None
        from anyfesolver import apply_imperfection

        return cls(model=apply_imperfection(base_model, imperfection_field, copy_model=True))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def model(self) -> Any:
        """The solved FE model; raises when this binding is a refusal."""

        if self._refusal is not None:
            raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(self._refusal)
        return self._model

    def __getstate__(self):
        return (self._model, self._refusal)

    def __setstate__(self, state) -> None:
        self._model, self._refusal = state


def _runtime_imperfection_unbound(view: Any) -> Optional[str]:
    records = _solver_imperfection_records(getattr(view, "raw_result", None))
    if records is not None:
        build_records = _records(getattr(view.built.fe_model, "imperfection_metadata", None))
        runtime = records[len(build_records):]
        if any(float(entry.get("max_offset", np.nan)) != 0.0 for entry in runtime):
            return "a run-time imperfection"
    info = getattr(view, "info", None)
    workflow = info.get("raw") if isinstance(info, Mapping) else None
    imperfect = getattr(workflow, "imperfect_model", None)
    if imperfect is not None and imperfect is not view.built.fe_model:
        field = getattr(workflow, "imperfection", None)
        if field is None or _has_offset(field):
            return "the capacity workflow's imperfection"
    return None


def unbound_refusal(view: Any) -> Optional[str]:
    """The refusal message for an imperfect result without a binding, if any."""

    reason = _runtime_imperfection_unbound(view)
    if reason is None:
        return None
    return (
        f"this result was solved on geometry moved by {reason}, but it carries "
        "no binding to that solved model; stresses recovered on the perfect "
        "built model would be wrong, so recovery is refused"
    )


def solved_model_of(view: Any) -> Any:

    Views without a binding use ``built.fe_model``, exactly as before -- unless
    their solver record says the solve ran on imperfect geometry that is not
    in ``built.fe_model``, which is refused rather than recovered on the
    perfect shape.
    """

    geometry = getattr(view, "solved_geometry", None)
    if geometry is not None:
        return geometry.model()
    refusal = unbound_refusal(view)
    if refusal is not None:

    ``field`` names what to colour by -- a displacement component or a stress
    component.  A per-node field is averaged onto each element for shading; a
    per-element field such as von Mises colours each element with its own
    value, which is where it actually lives.  ``values`` accepts a
    pre-computed :class:`~anyfem.post.fields.Field`, so an envelope can be
    drawn the same way a single shape is.
    """

    from ..post.fields import Field, evaluate_field, field_unit

    mesh = shape.built.mesh
    mesh_id = getattr(mesh, "_anyfem_mesh_record_id", None)
    name = field or component
    if values is None:
        resolved = evaluate_field(shape, name)
    elif isinstance(values, Field):
        resolved = values
        name = resolved.name
    else:
        raise TypeError("values must be a Field")

    ordered_nodes = sorted(mesh.nodes)
    try:
        positions = shape.deformed_positions(scale)
    except KeyError:
        # Imported stress-only files have no translation field.  They remain
        # valid contour results and are displayed on the undeformed mesh.
        positions = mesh.node_positions()
    deformed = {node: positions[index] for index, node in enumerate(ordered_nodes)}
    # Lightweight imported/test result adapters historically expose only a
    # mesh on ``built``.  Geometry ownership is additive: keep those adapters
    # renderable and simply omit a geometry PickOwner when no project exists.
    built_project = getattr(shape.built, "project", None)
    geometry = getattr(built_project, "geometry", None)

    from .result_display import unit_transform

    value_scale, display_unit = unit_transform(resolved.unit, display_units)
    finite_values = [
        float(value)
        for value in resolved.values.values()
        if np.isfinite(float(value))
    ]
    has_values = bool(finite_values)
    if limits is None:
        if has_values:
            raw_low, raw_high = min(finite_values), max(finite_values)
            if raw_high <= raw_low:
                raw_high = raw_low + 1.0
        else:
            raw_low, raw_high = 0.0, 1.0
        low, high = raw_low * value_scale, raw_high * value_scale
    else:
        low, high = limits
    if high <= low:
        high = low + 1.0
    span = high - low

    def colour(value: float) -> str:
        return _ramp_color((value * value_scale - low) / span, colormap)

    def nodal_average(node_ids: Sequence[int]) -> Optional[float]:
        """Average only a complete finite nodal value set.

        A result quantity may deliberately cover only part of a mixed mesh.
        Missing values are unavailable, not zero, and must not make result
        browsing fail merely because an adjacent element uses another result
        location or formulation.
        """

        samples = []
        for node_id in node_ids:
            value = resolved.node_values.get(int(node_id))
            if value is None or not np.isfinite(float(value)):
                return None
            samples.append(float(value))
        return float(np.mean(samples)) if samples else None

    scene = Scene()
    shells = mesh.shells
    for face_id, element_ids in sorted(mesh.elements_of_face.items()):
        polygons: List[np.ndarray] = []
        colors: List[str] = []
        rendered_ids: List[int] = []
        for element_id in element_ids:
            if element_id not in shells:
                continue
            nodes = shells[element_id]
            polygons.append(
                np.array(
                    [deformed[node] for node in mesh.corners_of(element_id)]
                )
            )
            rendered_ids.append(int(element_id))
            if resolved.per_element:
                value = resolved.element_values.get(element_id)
            else:
                value = nodal_average(nodes)
            colors.append(
                COLOR_MESH_FILL
                if value is None or not np.isfinite(float(value))
                else colour(float(value))
            )
        if polygons:
    legend_colors = (
        [colour(float(value) / value_scale) for value in np.linspace(low, high, 5)]
        if has_values
        else []
    )
    scene.legend = {
        "levels": list(np.linspace(low, high, 5)) if has_values else [],
        "unit": unit,
        "title": (
            _component_title(name)
            if has_values
            else f"{_component_title(name)} (unavailable)"
        ),
        "colors": legend_colors,
    }
    scene.result_field, scene.result_mesh = resolved, mesh
    if not resolved.per_element:
        scene.legend["title"] += " Â· element mean for display"
    scene.result_colormap = tuple(colormap or _DEFAULT_RAMP)
    scene.result_scalar_range = (low / value_scale, high / value_scale)
    return scene


def read_persisted_display_field(dataset,field_key,*,frame=0,component=None):
    from ..post.fields import Field
    stored = dataset.field(field_key)
    descriptor = stored.descriptor
    local_frame=_persisted_frame(stored,frame)
    if component is not None and component not in descriptor.components:
        raise ValueError(f"{descriptor.label} has no saved component {component!r}")
    if component is not None:
        values = stored.read_component(component,local_frame)
        scalar = _artifact_scalar_rows(values,(),None,descriptor.provenance.get("scalar_sample_axes"))
    else:
        values = stored.read(local_frame)
        scalar = _artifact_scalar_rows(values,descriptor.components,None,descriptor.provenance.get("scalar_sample_axes"))
    location = descriptor.location
    if location not in ("node", "element", "integration_point"):
        raise ValueError(f"{descriptor.label} is not a spatial contour quantity")
    table_kind = "node" if location == "node" else "element"
    table_key = f"{field_key}_{table_kind}_ids"
    if table_key not in dataset.table_keys:
        raise ValueError(f"{descriptor.label} has no persisted {table_kind} association")
    identifiers = np.asarray(dataset.table(table_key), dtype=int).reshape(-1)
    if len(scalar) != len(identifiers):
        raise ValueError(
            f"{descriptor.label} has {len(scalar)} values for {len(identifiers)} IDs"
        )
    mapping = {
        int(identifier): float(value)
        for identifier, value in zip(identifiers, scalar)
        if np.isfinite(value)
    }
    field = Field(
        name=field_key,
        unit=descriptor.unit,
        node_values=mapping if table_kind == "node" else {},
        element_values=mapping if table_kind == "element" else {},
        reduction="absolute single component" if component is None and len(descriptor.components)==1 and values.ndim>1 else descriptor.reduction,
    )

    return field,descriptor


_PERFECT_STAGES = frozenset({"reference_static", "elastic_buckling"})


def _reference_record(dataset):
    """``(kind, record)`` of the saved reference geometry; absent means the mesh."""

    try:
        provenance = dataset.metadata("provenance")
    except (KeyError, AttributeError):
        provenance = None
    if not isinstance(provenance, Mapping) or "reference_geometry" not in provenance:
        return "mesh_record", {}
    record = provenance["reference_geometry"]
    kind = record.get("kind") if isinstance(record, Mapping) else None
    if kind not in ("mesh_record", "solved_imperfect", "unbound_imperfect"):
        raise ValueError(f"unrecognised saved reference geometry {record!r}")
    return kind, record


def persisted_reference_positions(dataset, mesh, ordered_nodes, *, perfect_stage=False) -> np.ndarray:
    """Coordinates a saved result's displacements are measured from.

    The mesh record unless the artifact says it was solved on moved geometry,
    in which case the stored imperfect coordinates are used.  Artifacts written
    before the record existed have no key and read as the mesh.  An imperfect
    result whose geometry was not saved, or a record this reader does not
    understand, is refused.  ``perfect_stage`` marks a capacity stage that was
    solved on the unmoved model (reference static, elastic buckling).
    """

    kind, record = _reference_record(dataset)
    if kind == "unbound_imperfect":
        raise ValueError(
            "this result was solved on imperfect geometry that its artifact does not "
            "store; the deformed shape is not drawn on the perfect mesh"
        )
    if kind == "solved_imperfect" and not perfect_stage:
        ids = np.asarray(dataset.table(record["ids_table"]))
        stored = np.asarray(dataset.table(record["positions_table"]), dtype=float)
        if (
            ids.ndim != 1 or ids.dtype.kind not in "iu" or stored.shape != (len(ids), 3)
            or len(set(ids.tolist())) != len(ids) or not np.isfinite(stored).all()
        ):
            raise ValueError("the saved reference geometry is malformed; it is not used")
        row_of = {int(node): row for row, node in enumerate(ids)}
        try:
            return stored[[row_of[int(node)] for node in ordered_nodes]]
        except KeyError as error:
            raise ValueError(
                f"the saved reference geometry has no node {error.args[0]}; "
                "it does not match the result's mesh"
            ) from None
    return np.asarray([mesh.nodes[node] for node in ordered_nodes], dtype=float)


def build_persisted_result_scene(
    project: Project,
    mesh: Mesh,
    dataset,
    field_key: str,
    *,
    frame: int = 0,
    component: str | None = None,
    scale: float = 1.0,
    limits: Optional[tuple[float, float]] = None,
    colormap: Optional[Sequence[tuple[float, str]]] = None,
    display_units: str = "SI (m / Pa)",
    show_nodes: bool = False,
    show_beam_sections: bool = True,
) -> Scene:
    """Render one lazily-read artifact field without inventing quantities."""

    from ..post.fields import Field

    field,descriptor = read_persisted_display_field(dataset,field_key,frame=frame,component=component)
    ordered_nodes = sorted(mesh.nodes)
    perfect_stage = descriptor.provenance.get("capacity_stage") in _PERFECT_STAGES
    positions = persisted_reference_positions(
        dataset, mesh, ordered_nodes, perfect_stage=perfect_stage
    )
    # A stage solved on the unmoved model is not deformed by the collapse
    # displacement of a result whose later stages ran on moved geometry.
    deform = not (perfect_stage and _reference_record(dataset)[0] == "solved_imperfect")
    if deform and "displacement" in dataset.field_keys and scale != 0.0:
        displacement = dataset.field("displacement")
        displacement_key = "displacement_node_ids"
        if displacement_key in dataset.table_keys:
            displacement_values = np.asarray(
                displacement.read(_persisted_frame(displacement,frame)),
                dtype=float,
            )
            displacement_ids = np.asarray(
                dataset.table(displacement_key), dtype=int
            ).reshape(-1)
            labels=tuple(displacement.descriptor.components)
            if displacement_values.ndim >= 2 and {"ux","uy","uz"}<=set(labels):
                translation_indices=[labels.index(label) for label in ("ux","uy","uz")]
                by_node = {
                    int(node): np.take(row,translation_indices,axis=-1).reshape(-1, 3)[0]
                    for node, row in zip(displacement_ids, displacement_values)
                }
                positions = positions + scale * np.asarray(
                    [by_node.get(int(node), np.zeros(3)) for node in ordered_nodes],
                    dtype=float,
                ).reshape(-1, 3)

    shape = SimpleNamespace(
        built=SimpleNamespace(mesh=mesh, project=project),
        deformed_positions=lambda _scale=1.0: positions,
    )
    scene = build_result_scene(
        shape,
        field=field_key,
        scale=1.0,
        limits=limits,
        values=field,
        colormap=colormap,
        display_units=display_units,
        show_nodes=show_nodes,
        show_beam_sections=show_beam_sections,
    )
    _,_,scene.legend=persisted_display_metadata(field,descriptor,component,limits=limits,display_units=display_units,colormap=colormap)
    return scene

"""Display, probes and saved artifacts use the geometry the solve ran on.

A run-time (or capacity-workflow) imperfection moves the reference coordinates
of a copy of the model; the displacements are measured from that copy.  The
deformed shape must therefore be "imperfect + displacement", and a saved
artifact must carry that reference so reopening it draws the same shape.
Runs without an imperfection, and project imperfections applied at build time,
keep drawing on the mesh coordinates bit for bit.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from anyfem import eigenmode_imperfection, solve_capacity, solve_nonlinear_static
from anyfem.io.artifacts import ArtifactStore
from anyfem.io.result_artifact import write_solution_artifact
from anyfem.ui.scene import build_persisted_result_scene, build_result_scene

from test_imperfect_solved_geometry import (  # noqa: F401
    AMPLITUDE,
    compressed_plate,
    plate_and_mode,
)


def _fe_coordinates(model, node_ids):
    return np.array([[n.x, n.y, n.z] for n in (model.mesh.get_node(i) for i in node_ids)])


def _scene_positions(scene):
    return np.concatenate(
        [np.asarray(p, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 3) for patch in scene.faces for p in patch.polygons]
    )


def _runtime(plate_and_mode):
    project, mesh, buckling = plate_and_mode
    imperfection = eigenmode_imperfection(buckling, 1, amplitude=AMPLITUDE)
    return solve_nonlinear_static(
        project, mesh=mesh, imperfection=imperfection, max_load_factor=1.0, num_steps=1,
    )


def _capacity(plate_and_mode):
    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    return solve_capacity(
        project, mesh=mesh, num_buckling_modes=1, imperfection_amplitude=AMPLITUDE,
        num_steps=1, max_load_factor=1.0,
    )


def _store(tmp_path, solution):
    project = solution.built.project
    store = ArtifactStore(tmp_path / "plate.anyfem")
    identity = dict(mesh_id="mesh", document_id=project.document_id,
                    model_hash="model", mesh_hash="hash")
    store.write_mesh(solution.built.mesh, **identity)
    artifact = write_solution_artifact(store, solution, job_id="job", analysis_hash="a", **identity)
    return store, artifact


@pytest.mark.parametrize("route", ["nonlinear", "capacity"])
def test_deformed_positions_are_imperfect_reference_plus_displacement(plate_and_mode, route):
    solution = (_runtime if route == "nonlinear" else _capacity)(plate_and_mode)
    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
    reference = _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)
    assert np.max(np.abs(reference - solution.built.mesh.node_positions())) > 0.5 * AMPLITUDE

    np.testing.assert_array_equal(solution.reference_positions(), reference)
    expected = reference + 3.0 * solution.translations()
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(solution.deformed_positions(3.0), expected)

    scene = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
    by_node = dict(zip(ids, expected))
    first = next(iter(solution.built.mesh.shells))
    corners = np.array([by_node[n] for n in solution.built.mesh.corners_of(first)])
    polygons = [np.asarray(poly) for patch in scene.faces for poly in patch.polygons]
    assert any(poly.shape == corners.shape and np.array_equal(poly, corners) for poly in polygons)


def test_csv_positions_use_the_reference_geometry(plate_and_mode):
    from anyfem.post.report import field_to_csv

    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
    reference = dict(zip(ids, _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)))
    rows = [line.split(",") for line in field_to_csv(solution, "magnitude").splitlines()[1:]]
    assert rows
    for row in rows:
        np.testing.assert_allclose([float(v) for v in row[1:4]], reference[int(row[0])], atol=1e-8)


def test_artifact_records_the_reference_geometry_and_reopens_on_it(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
    reference = _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)

    assert dataset.metadata("provenance")["reference_geometry"]["kind"] == "solved_imperfect"
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.asarray(dataset.table("reference_node_ids")), ids)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.asarray(dataset.table("reference_node_positions")), reference)

    scene = build_persisted_result_scene(
        solution.built.project, solution.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=3.0
    )
    live = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(_scene_positions(scene), _scene_positions(live))


def test_ordinary_artifact_names_the_mesh_and_gains_no_table(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    solution = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
    assert dataset.metadata("provenance")["reference_geometry"]["kind"] == "mesh_record"
    assert "reference_node_positions" not in dataset.table_keys
    scene = build_persisted_result_scene(
        project, solution.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=3.0
    )
    live = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(_scene_positions(scene), _scene_positions(live))


def test_artifact_without_a_reference_record_reads_as_the_mesh(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    from anyfem.ui.scene import persisted_reference_positions

    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    solution = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)

    class Old:  # an artifact written before the reference record existed
        table_keys = tuple(k for k in dataset.table_keys if not k.startswith("reference_"))
        table = staticmethod(dataset.table)

        @staticmethod
        def metadata(name):
            old = dict(dataset.metadata(name))
            old.pop("reference_geometry", None)
            return old

    ordered = sorted(mesh.nodes)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
        persisted_reference_positions(Old, mesh, ordered), mesh.node_positions()
    )

"""Results, addressed the way the model was built.

The solver returns flat DOF vectors.  Postprocessing here re-attaches them to
the things the user actually placed -- points, lines and plates -- so a query
never has to know a node number.

Every analysis produces one or more *shapes*: a static deflection, a vibration
mode, a buckling mode, a step of a nonlinear path, an instant of a transient.
They all share :class:`ShapeView`, so anything that can display a static result
-- the scene builder, the results panel, the animation -- displays a mode or a
time step without knowing the difference.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Mapping, Optional, Tuple

import numpy as np

from anygeometry.entities import EntityRef
from ..solve.build import BuiltModel
from .solved_geometry import (
    SolvedGeometry,
    UnboundSolvedGeometryError,
    solved_model_of,
    solved_reference_positions,
)

__all__ = [
    "BucklingSolution",
    "CapacitySolution",
    "ImpactSolution",
    "ImportedSolution",
    "LinearBatchSolution",
    "LinearSolution",
    "LinearStaticBatchResult",
    "ModalSolution",
    "MultiShapeSolution",
    "NonlinearSolution",
    "NonlinearIncrementView",
    "ShapeView",
    "SolvedGeometry",
    "TransientSolution",
]

_DOF_INDEX = {"ux": 0, "uy": 1, "uz": 2, "rx": 3, "ry": 4, "rz": 5}


def submitted_plate_thicknesses(built) -> dict[int, float]:
    """Physical thickness in metres for the face assignments used in this solve."""
    project = getattr(built, "project", None)
    return {int(face): project.plate_sections[section].thickness
            for face, section in getattr(project, "face_sections", {}).items()}


@dataclass
class ShapeView:
    """One displacement field over the model.

    ``value`` carries whatever number the shape is indexed by -- a frequency, a
    buckling load factor, a load factor, a time -- so a caller can label it
    without knowing which analysis produced it.
    """

    displacements: np.ndarray
    built: BuiltModel
    label: str = "result"
    value: float = 0.0

    def retained_stress_recovery(self, kind: str):
        """Return a previously recovered stress bundle without invoking the solver."""
        attributes = {"native": "_stress", "global": "_requested_global_stress",
                      "patch": "_requested_patch_stress"}
        if kind not in attributes:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown retained stress recovery {kind!r}")
        return getattr(self, attributes[kind], None)

    def solved_model(self):
        """The FE model whose reference coordinates these displacements use.

        ``built.fe_model`` unless the solve ran on a copy moved by a run-time
        or capacity-workflow imperfection; see :mod:`anyfem.post.solved_geometry`.
        """

        return solved_model_of(self)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # displacement access
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def node_displacement(self, node_id: int) -> np.ndarray:
        """The six displacement components at one node."""

        dofs = self.built.fe_model.mesh.dof_manager.get_node_dofs(node_id)
        if not dofs:
            raise KeyError(f"node {node_id} is not in the solved model")
        return self.displacements[dofs]

    def displacement_at(self, ref: EntityRef) -> Dict[int, np.ndarray]:
        """Displacements of every node on one geometry entity."""

        return {
            node_id: self.node_displacement(node_id)
            for node_id in self.built.mesh.nodes_on(ref)
        }

    def connection_motion(self, connection: str) -> Dict[str, np.ndarray]:
        """Where a connection's reference point is and how it moved.

        ``connection`` is its name or id.  For a rigid connection this is the
        motion of the whole tied section; for a distributing one it is the
        weighted average motion of the tied nodes.  Rotations are in radians.
        """

        item = self.built.connections.get(connection)
        if item is None:
            item = next(
                (
                    candidate
                    for candidate in self.built.connections.values()
                    if candidate.connection.name == connection
                ),
                None,
            )
        if item is None:
            raise KeyError(f"no connection {connection!r} in the solved model")
        values = self.node_displacement(item.reference_node)
        motion = {
            "reference_point": np.asarray(item.reference, dtype=float).copy(),
            "displacement": np.asarray(values[:3], dtype=float),
            "rotation": np.asarray(values[3:], dtype=float),
        }
        if item.section_node is not None:
            # A spring connection: the loaded point and the section it is
            # sprung from move differently; the difference is the spring.
            section = self.node_displacement(item.section_node)
            motion["section_displacement"] = np.asarray(section[:3], dtype=float)
            motion["section_rotation"] = np.asarray(section[3:], dtype=float)
        return motion

    def point_displacement(self, ref: EntityRef) -> np.ndarray:
        """The six components at a modelled point."""

        if ref.kind != "vertex":
            raise ValueError("point_displacement expects a point reference")
        node_id = self.built.mesh.node_of_vertex.get(ref.id)
        if node_id is None:
            raise KeyError(f"{ref} has no node in the mesh")
        return self.node_displacement(node_id)

    def component(self, name: str) -> np.ndarray:
        """One displacement component for every node, ordered by node ID."""

        try:
            offset = _DOF_INDEX[name]
        except KeyError:
            raise ValueError(
                f"unknown component {name!r}; expected one of "
                f"{', '.join(_DOF_INDEX)}"
            ) from None
        manager = self.built.fe_model.mesh.dof_manager
        return np.array(
            [
                self.displacements[manager.get_node_dofs(node_id)[offset]]
                for node_id in sorted(self.built.mesh.nodes)
            ]
        )

    def translations(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """Translation vectors for every node, ordered by node ID."""

        manager = self.built.fe_model.mesh.dof_manager
        return np.array(
            [
                self.displacements[manager.get_node_dofs(node_id)[:3]]
                for node_id in sorted(self.built.mesh.nodes)
            ]
        )

    def max_translation(self) -> Tuple[int, float]:
        """The node with the largest translation magnitude, and that magnitude."""

        node_ids = sorted(self.built.mesh.nodes)
        magnitudes = np.linalg.norm(self.translations(), axis=1)
        index = int(np.argmax(magnitudes))
        return node_ids[index], float(magnitudes[index])

    def deformed_positions(self, scale: float = 1.0) -> np.ndarray:
        """Node positions displaced by ``scale`` times the translation."""

        return self.reference_positions() + scale * self.translations()

    def reference_positions(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """Node coordinates the displacements are measured from, by node ID.

        The mesh coordinates, unless the solve ran on a copy moved by a
        run-time or capacity-workflow imperfection; then the solved model's
        coordinates (see :mod:`anyfem.post.solved_geometry`).
        """

        moved = solved_reference_positions(self)
        return self.built.mesh.node_positions() if moved is None else moved


@dataclass
class ImportedSolution(ShapeView):
    """A result another solver produced, displayed through the same interface.

    Two things make it different from a solved one, and both are enforced
    rather than smoothed over.

    It knows **which components it has**.  A CalculiX FRD carries three
    translations and no rotations, and a rotation reported as zero is a
    plausible number and a wrong one, so asking for an absent component raises.
    The underlying array holds NaN there, so code that indexes it directly
    still cannot mistake the gap for an answer.

    Its **stresses came from the file**, not from a recovery here.  They are
    kept as ready-made fields; nothing recomputes them, and a node-valued
    stress stays node-valued so it is never confused with something this layer
    derived.
    """

    results: Any = None
    components: frozenset = field(default_factory=frozenset)
    fields: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    covered: int = 0
    label: str = "imported"

    def _require_translations(self) -> None:
        if not {"ux", "uy", "uz"}.issubset(self.components):
            raise KeyError(
                f"{self.label} carries no displacement field; this imported "
                "result contains stresses only"
            )

    def node_displacement(self, node_id: int) -> np.ndarray:
        self._require_translations()
        return super().node_displacement(node_id)

    def translations(self) -> np.ndarray:
        self._require_translations()
        return super().translations()

    def component(self, name: str) -> np.ndarray:
        if name in _DOF_INDEX and name not in self.components:
            available = ", ".join(sorted(self.components)) or "none"
            raise KeyError(
                f"{self.label} carries no {name!r}: this file format does not "
                f"store it. Available components are {available}. It is not "
                "zero -- it is absent."
            )
        return super().component(name)

    def available_fields(self) -> List[str]:
        """Displacement components plus whatever stresses the file named."""

        order = [name for name in _DOF_INDEX if name in self.components]
        displacement = ["magnitude"] + order if order else []
        return displacement + list(self.fields)

    def summary(self) -> str:
        pieces = [self.label, f"{self.covered} nodes matched"]
        if self.results is not None:
            pieces.append(self.results.summary())
        if {"ux", "uy", "uz"}.issubset(self.components):
            node_id, magnitude = self.max_translation()
            pieces.append(
                f"max translation {magnitude:.6g} m at node {node_id}"
            )

    ref: EntityRef
    name: str
    unit: str
    distances: np.ndarray
    values: np.ndarray
    positions: np.ndarray

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self.distances)

    @property
    def length(self) -> float:
        return 0.0 if len(self.distances) == 0 else float(self.distances[-1])

    def to_csv(self) -> str:
        header = f"distance_m,{self.name}" + (f"_{self.unit}" if self.unit else "")
        rows = [
            f"{distance:.9g},{value:.9g}"
            for distance, value in zip(self.distances, self.values)
        ]
        return "\n".join([header, *rows])


def along_line(
    shape,
    ref: EntityRef,
    name: str = "magnitude",
    *,
    reduction: str = "mean",
) -> PathResult:
    """Sample a field along a line, node by node.

    A per-element field has no value *at* a node, so each node takes the
    average of the elements meeting it there.  That is a stated choice, not a
    recovered nodal stress.
    """

    if ref.kind != "edge":
        raise ValueError("along_line expects a line reference")

    mesh = shape.built.mesh
    node_ids = mesh.nodes_on(ref)
    if not node_ids:
        raise KeyError(f"{ref} has no nodes in the mesh")

    from .solved_geometry import reference_position_map

    coordinates = reference_position_map(shape, mesh)
    positions = np.array([coordinates[node_id] for node_id in node_ids])
    steps = np.linalg.norm(np.diff(positions, axis=0), axis=1)
    distances = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(steps)))

    field = evaluate_field(shape, name, reduction=reduction)
    if field.per_element:
        attached = nodes_to_elements(mesh)
        on_line = set(mesh.elements_on(ref))
        values = []
        for node_id in node_ids:
            candidates = [
                element_id
                for element_id in attached.get(node_id, ())
                if element_id in field.element_values
                and (not on_line or element_id in on_line)
            ]
            if not candidates:
                candidates = [
                    element_id
                    for element_id in attached.get(node_id, ())
                    if element_id in field.element_values
                ]
            values.append(
                float(np.mean([field.element_values[e] for e in candidates]))
                if candidates
                else 0.0
            )
        values = np.array(values)
    else:
        values = np.array([field.node_values[node_id] for node_id in node_ids])

    return PathResult(
        ref=ref,
        name=name,
        unit=field.unit,
        distances=distances,
        values=values,
        positions=positions,
    )

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
@dataclass
class Envelope:
    """The extreme of a field over every shape of a result."""

    field: Field
    mode: str
    shape_of: Dict[int, int] = dataclass_field(default_factory=dict)
    shape_count: int = 0
    admitted_case_ids: tuple[str,...] = ()
    failed_case_ids: tuple[str,...] = ()

    @property
    def admitted_count(self):return len(self.admitted_case_ids)

    @property
    def failed_count(self):return len(self.failed_case_ids)

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return self.field.name

    def worst_shape(self) -> Optional[int]:
        """Which shape index produced the overall extreme."""

        key, _value = self.field.extreme()
        return None if key is None else self.shape_of.get(key)

        )
        _add_dof_field(
            builder,
            "displacement",
